step0_inspect_source():\n    wb = load_workbook(SOURCE_XLSX, data_only=True)\n    print(\"Step 0.1 — Sheet names:\", wb.sheetnames)\n\n    entries = pd.read_excel(SOURCE_XLSX, entries, rules\n\n\ndef build_formatted(entries: pd.DataFrame, rules: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    # Map/rename source columns to expected names.\n    # In this dataset they already match, but keep a mapping in case.\n    rename_map = {\n        \"Week Ending\": \"Week Ending\",\n        \"Employee ID\": \"Employee ID\",\n        \"Role\": \"Role\",\n        \"Hours Worked\": \"Hours Worked\",\n        \"Break Minutes\": \"Break Minutes\",\n        \"Approval Code\": \"Approval Code\",\n        \"Project Code\": \"Project Code\",\n        \"Manager\": \"Manager\",\n    }\n\n    missing = [c for c in rename_map.keys() if c not in entries.columns]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Entries missing required columns: {missing}\")\n\n    formatted = \"Hours Worked\",\n        \"Break Minutes\",\n        \"Approval Code\",\n        \"Project Code\",\n        \"Manager\",\n    ]]\n\n    # Build rules lookup\n    required_rule_cols = [\"Role\", \"Min Break Minutes\", \"Overtime Threshold\"]\n    for c in required_rule_cols:\n        if c not in rules.columns:\n            raise ValueError(f\"BreakRules missing required column: {c}\")\n\n    rules_lu = rules.set_index(\"Role\")[[\"Min Break Minutes\", \"Overtime Threshold\"]].to_dict(orient=\"index\")\n\n    def compute_row(row):\n        role = row[\"Role\"]\n        if role not in rules_lu:\n            raise ValueError(f\"No BreakRules entry for role={role!r}\")\n        min_break = rules_lu[role][\"Min Break Minutes\"]\n        ot_threshold = rules_lu[role][\"Overtime Threshold\"]\n\n        break_minutes = row[\"Break Minutes\"]\n        hours_worked = row[\"Hours Worked\"]\n        approval_code = row[\"Approval Code\"]\n\n        break_deficit = 1 if float(break_minutes) < float(min_break) else 0\n        approval_missing = 1 if (float(hours_worked) > float(ot_threshold) and _is_blank(approval_code)) else 0\n        total_errors = int(break_deficit + approval_missing)\n\n        if total_errors == 0:\n            summary = \"None\"\n        elif break_deficit == 1 and approval_missing == 0:\n            summary = \"Break Deficit\"\n        elif break_deficit == 0 and approval_missing == 1:\n            summary = \"Approval Missing\"\n        else:\n            summary = \"Break Deficit, Approval Missing\"\n\n        return pd.Series(\n            {\n                \"Break Deficit\": int(break_deficit),\n                \"Approval Missing\": int(approval_missing),\n                \"Total Errors\": int(total_errors),\n                \"Error Summary\": summary,\n            }\n        )\n\n    computed = formatted.apply(compute_row, axis=1)\n    formatted = pd.concat([formatted, computed], axis=1)\n\n    # Final column order validation\n    if list(formatted.columns) != REQUIRED_FORMATTED_HEADERS:\n        raise AssertionError(\n            \"Formatted Data columns do not match pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    grouped = (\n        formatted\n        .groupby([\"Employee ID\", \"Week Ending\"], as_index=False)\n        .agg({\n            \"Break Deficit\": \"sum\",\n            \"Approval Missing\": \"sum\",\n            \"Total Errors\": \"sum\",\n        })\n        .rename(columns={\n            \"Break Deficit\": \"Break Deficits\",\n            \"Approval Missing\": \"Approval Gaps\",\n        })\n    )\n\n    # Only groups where Total Errors > 0\n    grouped = grouped[grouped[\"Total Errors\"] > 0].copy()\n\n    # Sort\n    grouped = grouped.sort_values([\"Employee ID\", \"Week Ending\"], ascending=[True, True], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # Grand total row uses dataset-wide totals over *all* rows (not just error rows)\n    totals = {\n        \"Break Deficits\": int(formatted[\"Break Deficit\"].sum()),\n        \"Approval Gaps\": int(formatted[\"Approval Missing\"].sum()),\n        \"Total Errors\": int(formatted[\"Total Errors\"].sum()),\n    }\n\n    grand_total = pd.DataFrame([\n        {\n            \"Employee ID\": \"Grand Total\",\n            \"Week Ending\": \"-\",\n            **totals,\n        }\n    ])\n\n    summary = pd.concat([grouped[SUMMARY_HEADERS], grand_total[SUMMARY_HEADERS]], ignore_index=True)\n\n    # Validate headers\n    if list(summary.columns) != SUMMARY_HEADERS:\n        raise AssertionError(\n            \"Summary columns do not match contract.\\n\"\n            f\"Expected: {SUMMARY_HEADERS}\\n\"\n            f\"Actual:   {list(summary.columns)}\"\n        )\n\n    return summary\n\n\ndef write_excel(entries: pd.DataFrame, formatted: pd.DataFrame, summary: pd.DataFrame) -> None:\n    # Use pandas ExcelWriter with openpyxl engine to create multiple sheets\n    with pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[str, str]:\n    # Determine top-2 employees by total errors across all weeks (from Summary data excluding Grand Total)\n    summary_no_grand = summary_df[summary_df[\"Employee ID\"] != \"Grand Total\"].copy()\n\n    per_employee = (\n        summary_no_grand\n        .groupby(\"Employee ID\", as_index=False)\n        .agg({\"Total Errors\": \"sum\"})\n        .sort_values([\"Total Errors\", \"Employee ID\"], ascending=[False, True], kind=\"mergesort\")\n    )\n\n    top2 = per_employee.head(2)[\"Employee ID\"].tolist()\n    if len(top2) < 2:\n        # Gaps\"]\n    te = grand_totals[\"Total Errors\"]\n\n    exec_summary = (\n        \"This brief summarizes timesheet compliance issues identified in the audit period. \"\n        \"A Break Deficit \"Week Ending\",\n    \"Employee ID\",\n    \"Role\",\n    \"Hours Worked\",\n    \"Break Minutes\",\n    \"Approval Code\",\n    \"Project Code\",\n    \"Manager\",\n    \"Break Deficit\",\n    \"Approval Missing\",\n    \"Total Errors\",\n    \"Error Summary\",\n]\n\nSUMMARY_HEADERS = [\n    \"Employee ID\",\n    \"Week Ending\",\n    \"Break Deficits\",\n    \"Approval Gaps\",\n    \"Total Errors\",\n]\n\n\ndef _is_blank(value) -> bool:\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return True\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        return value.strip() == \"\"\n    return False\n\n\ndef step0_inspect_source():\n    wb = load_workbook(SOURCE_XLSX, data_only=True)\n    print(\"Step 0.1 — Sheet names:\", wb.sheetnames)\n\n    entries = pd.read_excel(SOURCE_XLSX, sheet_name=\"Entries\")\n    print(\"\\nStep 0.2 — Entries headers:\")\n    print(list(entries.columns))\n    print(\"\\nStep 0.2 — Entries first 5 rows:\")\n    print(entries.head(5).to_string(index=False))\n\n    rules = pd.read_excel(SOURCE_XLSX, sheet_name=\"BreakRules\")\n    print(\"\\nStep 0.3 — BreakRules headers:\")\n    print(list(rules.columns))\n    print(\"\\nStep 0.3 — BreakRules ALL rows:\")\n    print(rules.to_string(index=False))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 0.4 — Dtypes (Entries):\")\n    print(entries.dtypes)\n    if \"Approval Code\" in entries.columns:\n        print(\"Approval Code can be blank/NaN:\", bool(entries[\"Approval Code\"].isna().any()))\n    for col in [\"Hours Worked\", \"Break Minutes\"]:\n        if col in entries.columns:\n            print(f\"{col} is numeric?\", pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(entries[col]))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 0.4 — Dtypes (BreakRules):\")\n    print(rules.dtypes)\n\n    return entries, rules\n\n\ndef build_formatted(entries: pd.DataFrame, rules: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    # Map/rename source columns to expected names.\n    # In this dataset they already match, but keep a mapping in case.\n    rename_map = {\n        \"Week Ending\": \"Week Ending\",\n        \"Employee ID\": \"Employee ID\",\n        \"Role\": \"Role\",\n        \"Hours Worked\": \"Hours Worked\",\n        \"Break Minutes\": \"Break Minutes\",\n        \"Approval Code\": \"Approval Code\",\n        \"Project Code\": \"Project Code\",\n        \"Manager\": \"Manager\",\n    }\n\n    missing = [c for c in rename_map.keys() if c not in entries.columns]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Entries missing required columns: {missing}\")\n\n    formatted = entries.rename(columns=rename_map).copy()\n\n    # Ensure first 8 columns exactly in order.\n    formatted = formatted[[\n        \"Week Ending\",\n        \"Employee ID\",\n        \"Role\",\n        \"Hours Worked\",\n        \"Break Minutes\",\n        \"Approval Code\",\n        \"Project Code\",\n        \"Manager\",\n    ]]\n\n    # Build rules lookup\n    required_rule_cols = [\"Role\", \"Min Break Minutes\", \"Overtime Threshold\"]\n    for c in required_rule_cols:\n        if c not in rules.columns:\n            raise ValueError(f\"BreakRules missing required column: {c}\")\n\n    rules_lu = rules.set_index(\"Role\")[[\"Min Break Minutes\", \"Overtime Threshold\"]].to_dict(orient=\"index\")\n\n    def compute_row(row):\n        role = row[\"Role\"]\n        if role not in rules_lu:\n            raise ValueError(f\"No BreakRules entry for role={role!r}\")\n        min_break = rules_lu[role][\"Min Break Minutes\"]\n        ot_threshold = rules_lu[role][\"Overtime Threshold\"]\n\n        break_minutes = row[\"Break Minutes\"]\n        hours_worked = row[\"Hours Worked\"]\n        approval_code = row[\"Approval Code\"]\n\n        break_deficit = 1 if float(break_minutes) < float(min_break) else 0\n        approval_missing = 1 if (float(hours_worked) > float(ot_threshold) and _is_blank(approval_code)) else 0\n        total_errors = int(break_deficit + approval_missing)\n\n        if total_errors == 0:\n            summary = \"None\"\n        elif break_deficit == 1 and approval_missing == 0:\n            summary = \"Break Deficit\"\n        elif break_deficit == 0 and approval_missing == 1:\n            summary = \"Approval Missing\"\n        else:\n            summary = \"Break Deficit, Approval Missing\"\n\n        return pd.Series(\n            {\n                \"Break Deficit\": int(break_deficit),\n                \"Approval Missing\": int(approval_missing),\n                \"Total Errors\": int(total_errors),\n                \"Error Summary\": summary,\n            }\n        )\n\n    computed = formatted.apply(compute_row, axis=1)\n    formatted = pd.concat([formatted, computed], axis=1)\n\n    # Final column order validation\n    if list(formatted.columns) != REQUIRED_FORMATTED_HEADERS:\n        raise AssertionError(\n            \"Formatted Data columns do not match contract.\\n\"\n            f\"Expected: {REQUIRED_FORMATTED_HEADERS}\\n\"\n            f\"Actual:   {list(formatted.columns)}\"\n        )\n\n    return formatted\n\n\ndef build_summary(formatted: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    grouped = (\n        formatted\n        .groupby([\"Employee ID\", \"Week Ending\"], as_index=False)\n        .agg({\n            \"Break Deficit\": \"sum\",\n            \"Approval Missing\": \"sum\",\n            \"Total Errors\": \"sum\",\n        })\n        .rename(columns={\n            \"Break Deficit\": \"Break Deficits\",\n            \"Approval Missing\": \"Approval Gaps\",\n        })\n    )\n\n    # Only groups where Total Errors > 0\n    grouped = grouped[grouped[\"Total Errors\"] > 0].copy()\n\n    # Sort\n    grouped = grouped.sort_values([\"Employee ID\", \"Week Ending\"], ascending=[True, True], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # Grand total row uses dataset-wide totals over *all* rows (not just error rows)\n    totals = {\n        \"Break Deficits\": int(formatted[\"Break Deficit\"].sum()),\n        \"Approval Gaps\": int(formatted[\"Approval Missing\"].sum()),\n        \"Total Errors\": int(formatted[\"Total Errors\"].sum()),\n    }\n\n    grand_total = pd.DataFrame([\n        {\n            \"Employee ID\": \"Grand Total\",\n            \"Week Ending\": \"-\",\n            **totals,\n        }\n    ])\n\n    summary = pd.concat([grouped[SUMMARY_HEADERS], grand_total[SUMMARY_HEADERS]], ignore_index=True)\n\n    # Validate headers\n    if list(summary.columns) != SUMMARY_HEADERS:\n        raise AssertionError(\n            \"Summary columns do not match contract.\\n\"\n            f\"Expected: {SUMMARY_HEADERS}\\n\"\n            f\"Actual:   {list(summary.columns)}\"\n        )\n\n    return summary\n\n\ndef write_excel(entries: pd.DataFrame, formatted: pd.DataFrame, summary: pd.DataFrame) -> None:\n    # Use pandas ExcelWriter with openpyxl engine to create multiple sheets\n    with pd.ExcelWriter(OUT_AUDIT_XLSX, engine=\"openpyxl\") as writer:\n        entries.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=\"RawData\", index=False)\n        formatted.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=\"Formatted Data\", index=False)\n        summary.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=\"Summary\", index=False)\n\n\ndef step2_validate_excel() -> tuple[dict, list[str], pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:\n    wb = wb.sheetnames != expected_sheetnames:\n        raise AssertionError(f\"Sheet names must be exactly {expected_sheetnames}, got {wb.sheetnames}\")\n\n    # Validate formatted headers exactly\n    ws_f = print(formatted_headers)\n\n    if formatted_headers != REQUIRED_FORMATTED_HEADERS:\n        raise AssertionError(\n            \"Formatted Data headers do not match contract.\\n\"\n            f\"Expected: {REQUIRED_FORMATTED_HEADERS}\\n\"\n            f\"Actual:   {formatted_headers}\"\n        )\n\n    # Read back summary to check last row and totals\n    summary_df = Total'\")\n\n    grand = summary_df.iloc[-1]\n    grand_totals = {\n        \"Break Deficits\": int(grand[\"Break Deficits\"]),\n        \"Approval Gaps\": int(grand[\"Approval Gaps\"]),\n        \"Total Errors\": int(grand[\"Total Errors\"]),\n    }\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 2.3 — Grand Total values:\")\n    print(grand_totals)\n\n    formatted_df = pd.read_excel(OUT_AUDIT_XLSX, sheet_name=\"Formatted Data\")\n    print(\"\\nStep 2.4 — Formatted Data first 3 rows:\")\n    print(formatted_df.head(3).to_string(index=False))\n    print(\"\\nStep 2.4 — Formatted Data last 3 .sort_values([\"Total Errors\", \"Employee ID\"], ascending=[False, True], kind=\"mergesort\")\n    )\n\n    top2 = per_employee.head(2)[\"Employee ID\"].tolist()\n    if len(top2) < 2:\n        # Compliance Brief\", level=1)\n\n    # 3–6 sentences, must include definitions + totals + recommendation + top IDs.\n    bd = grand_totals[\"Break Deficits\"]\n    ag = grand_totals[\"Approval Gaps\"]\n    te = grand_totals[\"Total Errors\"]\n\n    exec_summary = (\n        \"This brief summarizes timesheet compliance issues identified in the audit period. \"\n        \"A Break Deficit occurs when an employee took fewer break minutes than the minimum required for their role. \"\n        \"An Approval Missing issue occurs when an employee worked more hours than the overtime threshold for their role without an approval code. \"\n        f\"Across all entries reviewed, we identified {bd} Break Deficits, {ag} Approval Gaps, and {te} Total Errors. \"\n        f\"The highest
